24.     /// <summary>
25.     /// An empty page that can be used on its own or navigated to within a Frame.
26.     /// </summary>
27.     public sealed partial class Scenario1_LaunchFile : Page
28.     {
29.         // A pointer back to the main page. This is needed if you want to call methods in MainPage such as NotifyUser().
30.         MainPage rootPage = MainPage.Current;
31. 
32.         string fileToLaunch = @"Assets\microsoft-sdk.png";
33. 
34.         public Scenario1_LaunchFile()
35.         {
36.             this.InitializeComponent();
37.             ViewPreference.ItemsSource = MainPage.ViewSizePreferences;
38.             ViewPreference.SelectedIndex = 0;
39.         }
40. 
41.         private async Task<StorageFile> GetFileToLaunch()
42.         {
43.             // First, get the image file from the package's image directory.
44.             var file = await Windows.ApplicationModel.Package.Current.InstalledLocation.GetFileAsync(fileToLaunch);
45. 
46.             //Can't launch files directly from install folder so copy it over 
47.             //to temporary folder first
48.             file = await file.CopyAsync(ApplicationData.Current.TemporaryFolder, "filetolaunch.png", NameCollisionOption.ReplaceExisting);
49. 
50.             return file;
51.         }
52. 
53.         /// <summary>
54.         // Launch a .png file that came with the package.
55.         /// </summary>
56.         private async void LaunchFileDefault()
57.         {
58.             // First, get the image file from the package's image directory.
59.             var file = await GetFileToLaunch();
60. 
61.             // Next, launch the file.
62.             bool success = await Launcher.LaunchFileAsync(file);
63.             if (success)
64.             {
65.                 rootPage.NotifyUser("File launched: " + file.Name, NotifyType.StatusMessage);
66.             }
67.             else
68.             {
69.                 rootPage.NotifyUser("File launch failed.", NotifyType.ErrorMessage);
70.             }
71.         }
72. 
73.         /// <summary>
74.         // Launch a .png file that came with the package. Show a warning prompt.
75.         /// </summary>
76.         private async void LaunchFileWithWarning()
77.         {
78.             // First, get the image file from the package's image directory.
79.             var file = await GetFileToLaunch();
80. 
81.             // Next, configure the warning prompt.
82.             var options = new LauncherOptions() { TreatAsUntrusted = true };
83. 
84.             // Finally, launch the file.
85.             bool success = await Launcher.LaunchFileAsync(file, options);
86.             if (success)
87.             {
88.                 rootPage.NotifyUser("File launched: " + file.Name, NotifyType.StatusMessage);
89.             }
90.             else
91.             {
92.                 rootPage.NotifyUser("File launch failed.", NotifyType.ErrorMessage);
93.             }
94.         }
95. 
96.         /// <summary>
97.         // Launch a .png file that came with the package. Show an Open With dialog that lets the user chose the handler to use.
98.         /// </summary>
99.         private async void LaunchFileOpenWith(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
100.         {
101.             // First, get the image file from the package's image directory.
102.             var file = await GetFileToLaunch();
103. 
104.             // Calculate the position for the Open With dialog.
105.             // An alternative to using the point is to set the rect of the UI element that triggered the launch.
106.             Point openWithPosition = MainPage.GetElementLocation(sender);
107. 
108.             // Next, configure the Open With dialog.
109.             var options = new LauncherOptions();
110.             options.DisplayApplicationPicker = true;
111.             options.UI.InvocationPoint = openWithPosition;
112.             options.UI.PreferredPlacement = Windows.UI.Popups.Placement.Below;
113. 
114.             // Finally, launch the file.
115.             bool success = await Launcher.LaunchFileAsync(file, options);
116.             if (success)
117.             {
118.                 rootPage.NotifyUser("File launched: " + file.Name, NotifyType.StatusMessage);
119.             }
120.             else
121.             {
122.                 rootPage.NotifyUser("File launch failed.", NotifyType.ErrorMessage);
123.             }
124.         }
125. 
126.         /// <summary>
127.         // Launch a .png file that came with the package. Request to share the screen with the launched app.
128.         /// </summary>
129.         private async void LaunchFileSplitScreen()
130.         {
131.             // First, get a file via the picker.
132.             // TODO[migrate-003]: see PATTERNS.md#pickers
133.             var openPicker = new Windows.Storage.Pickers.FileOpenPicker();
134.             openPicker.FileTypeFilter.Add("*");
135. 
136.             Windows.Storage.StorageFile file = await openPicker.PickSingleFileAsync();
137.             if (file != null)
138.             {
139.                 // Configure the request for split screen launch.
140.                 var options = new LauncherOptions() { DesiredRemainingView = (ViewSizePreference)ViewPreference.SelectedValue };
141. 
142.                 // Next, launch the file.
143.                 bool success = await Launcher.LaunchFileAsync(file, options);
144.                 if (success)
145.                 {
146.                     rootPage.NotifyUser("File launched: " + file.Name, NotifyType.StatusMessage);
147.                 }
148.                 else
149.                 {
150.                     rootPage.NotifyUser("File launch failed.", NotifyType.ErrorMessage);
151.                 }
152.             }
153.             else
154.             {
155.                 rootPage.NotifyUser("No file was picked.", NotifyType.ErrorMessage);
156.             }
157.         }
158. 
159.         /// <summary>
160.         // Have the user pick a file, then launch it.
161.         /// </summary>
162.         private async void PickAndLaunchFile()
163.         {
164.             // First, get a file via the picker.
165.             // TODO[migrate-004]: see PATTERNS.md#pickers
166.             var openPicker = new Windows.Storage.Pickers.FileOpenPicker();
167.             openPicker.FileTypeFilter.Add("*");
168.             Windows.Storage.StorageFile file = await openPicker.PickSingleFileAsync();
169.             if (file != null)
170.             {
171.                 // Next, launch the file.
172.                 bool success = await Launcher.LaunchFileAsync(file);
173.                 if (success)
174.                 {
175.                     rootPage.NotifyUser("File launched: " + file.Name, NotifyType.StatusMessage);
176.                 }
177.                 else
178.                 {
179.                     rootPage.NotifyUser("File launch failed.", NotifyType.ErrorMessage);
180.                 }
181.             }
182.             else
183.             {
184.                 rootPage.NotifyUser("No file was picked.", NotifyType.ErrorMessage);
185.             }
186.         }
187. 
188.         /// <summary>
189.         // The Open With dialog should be displayed just under the element that triggered it.
190.         /// </summary>
191.         private Windows.Foundation.Point GetOpenWithPosition(FrameworkElement element)
192.         {
193.             Microsoft.UI.Xaml.Media.GeneralTransform buttonTransform = element.TransformToVisual(null);
194. 
195.             Point desiredLocation = buttonTransform.TransformPoint(new Point());
196.             desiredLocation.Y = desiredLocation.Y + element.ActualHeight;
197. 
198.             return desiredLocation;
199.         }
200.     }

22.     public partial class MainPage : Page
23.     {
24.         public const string FEATURE_NAME = "Association Launching C# Sample";
25. 
26.         List<Scenario> scenarios = new List<Scenario>
27.         {
28.             new Scenario() { Title = "Launching a file", ClassType = typeof(Scenario1_LaunchFile) },
29.             new Scenario() { Title = "Launching a URI", ClassType = typeof(Scenario2_LaunchUri) },
30.             new Scenario() { Title = "Receiving a file", ClassType = typeof(Scenario3_ReceiveFile) },
31.             new Scenario() { Title = "Receiving a URI", ClassType = typeof(Scenario4_ReceiveUri) }

34.         static public List<ViewSizePreference> ViewSizePreferences = new List<ViewSizePreference>
35.         {
36.             ViewSizePreference.Default,
37.             ViewSizePreference.UseLess,
38.             ViewSizePreference.UseHalf,
39.             ViewSizePreference.UseMore,
40.             ViewSizePreference.UseMinimum,
41.             ViewSizePreference.UseNone,

44.         public void NavigateToPageWithParameter(int pageIndex, object parameter)
45.         {
46.             ScenarioControl.SelectedIndex = pageIndex;
47.             ScenarioFrame.Navigate(scenarios[pageIndex].ClassType, parameter);
48.         }
49. 
50.         static public Point GetElementLocation(object e)
51.         {
52.             var element = (FrameworkElement)e;
53.             Microsoft.UI.Xaml.Media.GeneralTransform buttonTransform = element.TransformToVisual(null);
54. 
55.             Point desiredLocation = buttonTransform.TransformPoint(new Point());
56.             desiredLocation.Y = desiredLocation.Y + element.ActualHeight;
57. 
58.             return desiredLocation;
59.         }
60.     }

63.     {
64.         public string Title { get; set; }
65. 
66.         public Type ClassType { get; set; }
67. 
68.         public override string ToString()
69.         {
70.             return Title;
71.         }
72.     }

74.     public partial class App : Application
75.     {
76.         /// <summary>
77.         // Handle file activations.
78.         /// </summary>
79.         protected override void OnFileActivated(FileActivatedEventArgs e)
80.         {
81.             Frame rootFrame = CreateRootFrame();
82. 
83.             if (rootFrame.Content == null)
84.             {
85.                 if (!rootFrame.Navigate(typeof(MainPage)))
86.                 {
87.                     throw new Exception("Failed to create initial page");
88.                 }
89.             }
90. 
91.             var p = rootFrame.Content as MainPage;
92.             p.NavigateToPageWithParameter(2, e);
93. 
94.             // Ensure the current window is active
95.             // TODO[migrate-001]: see PATTERNS.md#windowing
96.             Window.Current.Activate();
97.         }
98. 
99.         /// <summary>
100.         // Handle protocol activations.
101.         /// </summary>
102.         protected override void OnActivated(IActivatedEventArgs e)
103.         {
104.             if (e.Kind == ActivationKind.Protocol)
105.             {
106.                 Frame rootFrame = CreateRootFrame();
107. 
108.                 if (rootFrame.Content == null)
109.                 {
110.                     if (!rootFrame.Navigate(typeof(MainPage)))
111.                     {
112.                         throw new Exception("Failed to create initial page");
113.                     }
114.                 }
115. 
116.                 var p = rootFrame.Content as MainPage;
117.                 p.NavigateToPageWithParameter(3, e);
118. 
119.                 // Ensure the current window is active
120.                 // TODO[migrate-002]: see PATTERNS.md#windowing
121.                 Window.Current.Activate();
122.             }
123.         }
124. 
125.     }
126. }

<a id="windowing"></a>
## Windowing: Window.Current / ApplicationView / CoreWindow → AppWindow`Window.Current`, `ApplicationView`, and `CoreWindow` are gone. Track the main window yourself and use `Microsoft.UI.Windowing.AppWindow`. See the official [windowing guide](https://learn.microsoft.com/windows/apps/windows-app-sdk/migrate-to-windows-app-sdk/guides/windowing).UWP (replace this):```csharp
var win = Window.Current;
ApplicationView.GetForCurrentView().TryResizeView(new Size(800, 600));
```WinUI 3 — expose `MainWindow` from `App`:```csharp
public partial class App : Application
{
    // With <Nullable>enable</Nullable>, declare as nullable since the assignment only happens during OnLaunched.
    public static Window? MainWindow { get; private set; }    protected override void OnLaunched(LaunchActivatedEventArgs args)
    {
        var window = new MainWindow();
        MainWindow = window;
        window.Activate();
    }
}
```Consumers running after `OnLaunched` can safely null-forgive:```csharp
var hwnd = WinRT.Interop.WindowNative.GetWindowHandle(App.MainWindow!);
```Resize via `AppWindow`:```csharp
var hwnd = WinRT.Interop.WindowNative.GetWindowHandle(App.MainWindow);
var windowId = Microsoft.UI.Win32Interop.GetWindowIdFromWindow(hwnd);
var appWindow = AppWindow.GetFromWindowId(windowId);
appWindow.Resize(new SizeInt32(800, 600));
```### Initialization order — keep MainWindow's constructor inert`App.MainWindow` (or any equivalent static window reference) is `null` for the entire duration of `new MainWindow()` — the right-hand side runs to completion BEFORE the assignment happens. Anything inside that constructor that reads the static — directly or transitively — sees `null` and throws `E_POINTER` (0x80004003) at startup. The build is clean; the analyzer is silent; only a runtime launch reveals it.APIs that commonly trip this race when called from a `Page` reached during `MainWindow` construction:- `WinRT.Interop.WindowNative.GetWindowHandle(App.MainWindow)`
- `XamlRoot` / `Content.XamlRoot` access through a static-window helper
- `PrintManagerInterop.GetForWindow(hwnd)` / `ShowPrintUIForWindowAsync(hwnd)`
- `FileOpenPicker` / `FileSavePicker` / `FolderPicker` (require an HWND)
- `ContentDialog.ShowAsync()` when the dialog's `XamlRoot` is sourced from a static
- `AppWindow.GetFromWindowId(...)` chained through `App.MainWindow`
- Any custom title bar / `SetTitleBar` helper that resolves the HWND staticallySafe pattern — assign before activate, defer first navigation:```csharp
// App.xaml.cs
protected override void OnLaunched(LaunchActivatedEventArgs args)
{
    var window = new MainWindow();
    MainWindow = window;
    window.Activate();
}
```

The `MainWindow` constructor must do only `InitializeComponent` and inert setup — no navigation to a `Page` that reads `
`App.MainWindow`. Defer the first navigation until after `Activate` completes, either from a `Loaded` handler hooked in `
`InitializeComponent` or by exposing a `NavigateToInitialPage()` method and calling it from `App.OnLaunched` after the st
tatic is assigned.

```csharp
// MainWindow.xaml.cs
public MainWindow()
{
    InitializeComponent();
    this.Activated += (_, _) =>
    {
        if (!_navigated)
        {
            _navigated = true;
            RootFrame.Navigate(typeof(MainPage));
        }
    };
}
private bool _navigated;
```

Validator catches this race with a 10s smoke launch after the build healthcheck passes — see `Validate-UwpMigration.ps1`
` Section 7.

### AppWindow API replacements

| UWP API | WinUI 3 API |
|---------|-------------|
| `ApplicationView.TryResizeView` | `AppWindow.Resize` |
| `AppWindow.TryCreateAsync` | `AppWindow.Create` |
| `AppWindow.TryShowAsync` | `AppWindow.Show` |
| `AppWindow.TryConsolidateAsync` | `AppWindow.Destroy` |
| `AppWindow.RequestMoveXxx` | `AppWindow.Move` |
| `AppWindow.RequestPresentation` | `AppWindow.SetPresenter` |
| `CoreApplicationViewTitleBar` | `AppWindowTitleBar` |
| `CoreApplicationView.TitleBar.ExtendViewIntoTitleBar` | `AppWindow.TitleBar.ExtendsContentIntoTitleBar` |
---
<a id="pickers"></a>
## Pickers and Win32 Surfaces

UWP pickers infer the active window. WinUI 3 desktop pickers must be initialized explicitly or `ShowAsync` throws `COMEx
xception`.

```csharp
var picker = new FileOpenPicker();
picker.FileTypeFilter.Add(".txt");
var hwnd = WinRT.Interop.WindowNative.GetWindowHandle(App.MainWindow);
WinRT.Interop.InitializeWithWindow.Initialize(picker, hwnd);
var file = await picker.PickSingleFileAsync();
```

Apply the same `InitializeWithWindow.Initialize(obj, hwnd)` pattern to `FolderPicker`, `FileSavePicker`, `DataTransferMa
anager` (Share), `PrintManager`, and other UI surfaces that target a window.
<exited with exit code 0>

12. using System;
13. using System.Collections.Generic;
14. using Windows.UI.Core;
15. using Windows.UI.Xaml;
16. using Windows.UI.Xaml.Automation.Peers;
17. using Windows.UI.Xaml.Controls;
18. using Windows.UI.Xaml.Data;
19. using Windows.UI.Xaml.Media;
20. using Windows.UI.Xaml.Navigation;
21. 
22. // The Blank Page item template is documented at http://go.microsoft.com/fwlink/?LinkId=402352&clcid=0x409
23. 
24. namespace SDKTemplate
25. {
26.     /// <summary>
27.     /// An empty page that can be used on its own or navigated to within a Frame.
28.     /// </summary>
29.     public sealed partial class MainPage : Page
30.     {
31.         public static MainPage Current;
32. 
33.         public MainPage()
34.         {
35.             this.InitializeComponent();
36. 
37.             // This is a static public property that allows downstream pages to get a handle to the MainPage instance
38.             // in order to call methods that are in this class.
39.             Current = this;
40.             SampleTitle.Text = FEATURE_NAME;
41.         }
42. 
43.         protected override void OnNavigatedTo(NavigationEventArgs e)
44.         {
45.             // Populate the scenario list from the SampleConfiguration.cs file
46.             var itemCollection = new List<Scenario>();
47.             int i = 1;
48.             foreach (Scenario s in scenarios)
49.             {
50.                 itemCollection.Add(new Scenario { Title = $"{i++}) {s.Title}", ClassType = s.ClassType });
51.             }
52.             ScenarioControl.ItemsSource = itemCollection;
53. 
54.             if (Window.Current.Bounds.Width < 640)
55.             {
56.                 ScenarioControl.SelectedIndex = -1;
57.             }
58.             else
59.             {
60.                 ScenarioControl.SelectedIndex = 0;
61.             }
62.         }
63. 
64.         /// <summary>
65.         /// Called whenever the user changes selection in the scenarios list.  This method will navigate to the respective
66.         /// sample scenario page.
67.         /// </summary>
68.         /// <param name="sender"></param>
69.         /// <param name="e"></param>
70.         private void ScenarioControl_SelectionChanged(object sender, SelectionChangedEventArgs e)
71.         {
72.             // Clear the status block when navigating scenarios.
73.             NotifyUser(String.Empty, NotifyType.StatusMessage);
74. 
75.             ListBox scenarioListBox = sender as ListBox;
76.             Scenario s = scenarioListBox.SelectedItem as Scenario;
77.             if (s != null)
78.             {
79.                 ScenarioFrame.Navigate(s.ClassType);
80.                 if (Window.Current.Bounds.Width < 640)
81.                 {
82.                     Splitter.IsPaneOpen = false;
83.                 }
84.             }
85.         }
86. 
87.         public List<Scenario> Scenarios
88.         {
89.             get { return this.scenarios; }
90.         }
91. 
92.         /// <summary>
93.         /// Display a message to the user.
94.         /// This method may be called from any thread.
95.         /// </summary>
96.         /// <param name="strMessage"></param>
97.         /// <param name="type"></param>
98.         public void NotifyUser(string strMessage, NotifyType type)
99.         {
100.             // If called from the UI thread, then update immediately.
101.             // Otherwise, schedule a task on the UI thread to perform the update.
102.             if (Dispatcher.HasThreadAccess)
103.             {
104.                 UpdateStatus(strMessage, type);
105.             }
106.             else
107.             {
108.                 var task = Dispatcher.RunAsync(CoreDispatcherPriority.Normal, () => UpdateStatus(strMessage, type));
109.             }
110.         }
111. 
112.         private void UpdateStatus(string strMessage, NotifyType type)
113.         {
114.             switch (type)
115.             {
116.                 case NotifyType.StatusMessage:
117.                     StatusBorder.Background = new SolidColorBrush(Windows.UI.Colors.Green);
118.                     break;
119.                 case NotifyType.ErrorMessage:
120.                     StatusBorder.Background = new SolidColorBrush(Windows.UI.Colors.Red);
121.                     break;
122.             }
123. 
124.             StatusBlock.Text = strMessage;
125. 
126.             // Collapse the StatusBlock if it has no text to conserve real estate.
127.             StatusBorder.Visibility = (StatusBlock.Text != String.Empty) ? Visibility.Visible : Visibility.Collapsed;
128.             if (StatusBlock.Text != String.Empty)
129.             {
130.                 StatusBorder.Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
131.                 StatusPanel.Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
132.             }
133.             else
134.             {
135.                 StatusBorder.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
136.                 StatusPanel.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
137.             }
138. 
139. 			// Raise an event if necessary to enable a screen reader to announce the status update.
140. 			var peer = FrameworkElementAutomationPeer.FromElement(StatusBlock);
141. 			if (peer != null)
142. 			{
143. 				peer.RaiseAutomationEvent(AutomationEvents.LiveRegionChanged);
144. 			}
145. 		}
146. 
147.         async void Footer_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
148.         {
149.             await Windows.System.Launcher.LaunchUriAsync(new Uri(((HyperlinkButton)sender).Tag.ToString()));
150.         }
151. 
152.         private void Button_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
153.         {
154.             Splitter.IsPaneOpen = !Splitter.IsPaneOpen;
155.         }
156.     }
157.     public enum NotifyType
158.     {
159.         StatusMessage,
160.         ErrorMessage
161.     };
162. }

18. using Microsoft.UI.Xaml.Navigation;

20. namespace SDKTemplate
21. {
22.     /// <summary>
23.     /// An empty page that can be used on its own or navigated to within a Frame.
24.     /// </summary>
25.     public sealed partial class Scenario2_LaunchUri : Page
26.     {
27.         // A pointer back to the main page. This is needed if you want to call methods in MainPage such as NotifyUser()
28.         MainPage rootPage = MainPage.Current;
29. 
30.         public Scenario2_LaunchUri()
31.         {
32.             this.InitializeComponent();
33.             ViewPreference.ItemsSource = MainPage.ViewSizePreferences;
34.             ViewPreference.SelectedIndex = 0;
35.         }
36. 
37.         /// <summary>
38.         // Launch a URI.
39.         /// </summary>
40.         private async void LaunchUriDefault()
41.         {
42.             // Create the URI to launch from a string.
43.             var uri = new Uri(UriToLaunch.Text);
44. 
45.             // Launch the URI.
46.             bool success = await Launcher.LaunchUriAsync(uri);
47.             if (success)
48.             {
49.                 rootPage.NotifyUser("URI launched: " + uri.AbsoluteUri, NotifyType.StatusMessage);
50.             }
51.             else
52.             {
53.                 rootPage.NotifyUser("URI launch failed.", NotifyType.ErrorMessage);
54.             }
55.         }
56. 
57.         /// <summary>
58.         // Launch a URI. Show a warning prompt.
59.         /// </summary>
60.         private async void LaunchUriWithWarning()
61.         {
62.             // Create the URI to launch from a string.
63.             var uri = new Uri(UriToLaunch.Text);
64. 
65.             // Configure the warning prompt.
66.             var options = new LauncherOptions() { TreatAsUntrusted = true };
67. 
68.             // Launch the URI.
69.             bool success = await Launcher.LaunchUriAsync(uri, options);
70.             if (success)
71.             {
72.                 rootPage.NotifyUser("URI launched: " + uri.AbsoluteUri, NotifyType.StatusMessage);
73.             }
74.             else
75.             {
76.                 rootPage.NotifyUser("URI launch failed.", NotifyType.ErrorMessage);
77.             }
78.         }
79. 
80.         /// <summary>
81.         // Launch a URI. Show an Open With dialog that lets the user chose the handler to use.
82.         /// </summary>
83.         private async void LaunchUriOpenWith(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
84.         {
85.             // Create the URI to launch from a string.
86.             var uri = new Uri(UriToLaunch.Text);
87. 
88.             // Calulcate the position for the Open With dialog.
89.             // An alternative to using the point is to set the rect of the UI element that triggered the launch.
90.             Point openWithPosition = MainPage.GetElementLocation(sender);
91. 
92.             // Next, configure the Open With dialog.
93.             var options = new LauncherOptions();
94.             options.DisplayApplicationPicker = true;
95.             options.UI.InvocationPoint = openWithPosition;
96.             options.UI.PreferredPlacement = Windows.UI.Popups.Placement.Below;
97. 
98.             // Launch the URI.
99.             bool success = await Launcher.LaunchUriAsync(uri, options);
100.             if (success)
101.             {
102.                 rootPage.NotifyUser("URI launched: " + uri.AbsoluteUri, NotifyType.StatusMessage);
103.             }
104.             else
105.             {
106.                 rootPage.NotifyUser("URI launch failed.", NotifyType.ErrorMessage);
107.             }
108.         }
109. 
110.         /// <summary>
111.         // Launch a URI. Request to share the screen with the launched app.
112.         /// </summary>
113.         private async void LaunchUriSplitScreen()
114.         {
115.             // Create the URI to launch from a string.
116.             var uri = new Uri(UriToLaunch.Text);
117. 
118.             // Configure the request for split screen launch.
119.             var options = new LauncherOptions() { DesiredRemainingView = (ViewSizePreference)ViewPreference.SelectedValue };
120. 
121.             // Launch the URI.
122.             bool success = await Launcher.LaunchUriAsync(uri, options);
123.             if (success)
124.             {
125.                 rootPage.NotifyUser("URI launched: " + uri.AbsoluteUri, NotifyType.StatusMessage);
126.             }
127.             else
128.             {
129.                 rootPage.NotifyUser("URI launch failed.", NotifyType.ErrorMessage);
130.             }
131.         }
132.     }

16. using Windows.Storage;
17. using Microsoft.UI.Xaml;
18. using Microsoft.UI.Xaml.Controls;
19. using Microsoft.UI.Xaml.Navigation;
20. 
21. namespace SDKTemplate
22. {
23.     /// <summary>
24.     /// An empty page that can be used on its own or navigated to within a Frame.
25.     /// </summary>
26.     public sealed partial class Scenario3_ReceiveFile : Page
27.     {
28.         // A pointer back to the main page.  This is needed if you want to call methods in MainPage such
29.         // as NotifyUser()
30.         MainPage rootPage = MainPage.Current;
31. 
32.         string Extension = "alsdkcs";
33. 
34.         public Scenario3_ReceiveFile()
35.         {
36.             this.InitializeComponent();
37.         }
38. 
39.         /// <summary>
40.         /// Invoked when this page is about to be displayed in a Frame.
41.         /// </summary>
42.         /// <param name="e">Event data that describes how this page was reached.  The Parameter
43.         /// property is typically used to configure the page.</param>
44.         protected override async void OnNavigatedTo(NavigationEventArgs e)
45.         {
46.             // Display the result of the file activation if we got here as a result of being activated for a file.
47.             var args = e.Parameter as FileActivatedEventArgs;
48. 
49.             if (args != null)
50.             {
51.                 string output = "Files received: " + args.Files.Count + "\n";
52.                 foreach (StorageFile file in args.Files)
53.                 {
54.                     output = output + file.Name + "\n";
55.                 }
56. 
57.                 if (args.NeighboringFilesQuery != null)
58.                 {
59.                     // If supported, we receive the pictures from the same folder as the file that was launched
60.                     // because we declared the picturesLibrary capability. The order of the neighboring
61.                     // files matches the sort order of the folder from which the file was launched.
62.                     IReadOnlyList<StorageFile> neighboringFiles = await args.NeighboringFilesQuery.GetFilesAsync();
63.                     if (neighboringFiles.Count > 0)
64.                     {
65.                         output = output + "\nNeighboring files: " + neighboringFiles.Count + "\n";
66.                         int i;
67.                         for (i = 0; i < Math.Min(neighboringFiles.Count, 3); i++)
68.                         {
69.                             output = output + neighboringFiles[i].Name + "\n";
70.                         }
71.                         int remaining = neighboringFiles.Count - i;
72.                         if (remaining > 0)
73.                         {
74.                             output = output + "and " + remaining + " more.";
75.                         }
76.                     }
77.                 }
78.                 rootPage.NotifyUser(output, NotifyType.StatusMessage);
79.             }
80.         }
81. 
82.         private async void CreateTestFile()
83.         {
84.             StorageFolder folder = await KnownFolders.GetFolderAsync(KnownFolderId.PicturesLibrary);
85.             await folder.CreateFileAsync("Test " + Extension + " file." + Extension, CreationCollisionOption.ReplaceExisting);
86.             await Windows.System.Launcher.LaunchFolderAsync(folder);
87.         }
88. 
89.         private async void CreateTestFileWithNoExtension()
90.         {
91.             StorageFolder folder = await KnownFolders.GetFolderAsync(KnownFolderId.PicturesLibrary);
92.             await folder.CreateFileAsync("Test file with no extension", CreationCollisionOption.ReplaceExisting);
93.             await Windows.System.Launcher.LaunchFolderAsync(folder);
94.         }
95. 
96.         private async void RemoveTestFiles()
97.         {
98.             StorageFolder folder = await KnownFolders.GetFolderAsync(KnownFolderId.PicturesLibrary);
99.             try
100.             {
101.                 StorageFile file = await folder.GetFileAsync("Test " + Extension + " file." + Extension);
102.                 await file.DeleteAsync();
103.             }
104.             catch (Exception)
105.             {
106.                 // File I/O errors are reported as exceptions.
107.                 // Ignore errors here.
108.             }
109. 
110.             try
111.             {
112.                 StorageFile file = await folder.GetFileAsync("Test file with no extension");
113.                 await file.DeleteAsync();
114.             }
115.             catch (Exception)
116.             {
117.                 // File I/O errors are reported as exceptions.
118.                 // Ignore errors here.
119.             }
120.         }
121.     }
122. }

36.         /// </summary>
37.         /// <param name="e">Event data that describes how this page was reached. The Parameter

40.         {

42.

39.         /// will be used such as when the application is launched to open a specific file.
40.         /// </summary>
41.         /// <param name="e">Details about the launch request and process.</param>
42.         protected override void OnLaunched(LaunchActivatedEventArgs e)
43.         {
44. 
45. #if DEBUG
46.             if (System.Diagnostics.Debugger.IsAttached)
47.             {
48.                 this.DebugSettings.EnableFrameRateCounter = false;
49.             }
50. #endif
51. 
52.             Frame rootFrame = Window.Current.Content as Frame;
53. 
54.             // Do not repeat app initialization when the Window already has content,
55.             // just ensure that the window is active
56.             if (rootFrame == null)
57.             {
58.                 // Create a Frame to act as the navigation context and navigate to the first page
59.                 rootFrame = new Frame();
60.                 // Set the default language
61.                 rootFrame.Language = Windows.Globalization.ApplicationLanguages.Languages[0];
62. 
63.                 rootFrame.NavigationFailed += OnNavigationFailed;
64. 
65.                 if (e.PreviousExecutionState == ApplicationExecutionState.Terminated)
66.                 {
67.                     //TODO: Load state from previously suspended application
68.                 }
69. 
70.                 // Place the frame in the current Window
71.                 Window.Current.Content = rootFrame;
72.             }
73. 
74.             if (rootFrame.Content == null)
75.             {
76.                 // When the navigation stack isn't restored navigate to the first page,
77.                 // configuring the new page by passing required information as a navigation
78.                 // parameter
79.                 rootFrame.Navigate(typeof(MainPage), e.Arguments);
80.             }
81. 
82.             // Ensure the current window is active
83.             Window.Current.Activate();
84. 
85.             // Some samples want access to the LaunchActivatedEventArgs.
86.             LaunchCompleted(e);
87.         }
88. 
89.         private Frame CreateRootFrame()
90.         {
91.             Frame rootFrame = Window.Current.Content as Frame;
92. 
93.             // Do not repeat app initialization when the Window already has content,
94.             // just ensure that the window is active
95.             if (rootFrame == null)
96.             {
97.                 // Create a Frame to act as the navigation context and navigate to the first page
98.                 rootFrame = new Frame();
99. 
100.                 // Set the default language
101.                 rootFrame.Language = Windows.Globalization.ApplicationLanguages.Languages[0];
102.                 rootFrame.NavigationFailed += OnNavigationFailed;
103. 
104.                 // Place the frame in the current Window
105.                 Window.Current.Content = rootFrame;
106.             }
107. 
108.             return rootFrame;
109.         }

code:\n```csharp\n//*********************************************************\n//\n// Copyright (c) Microsoft. All rights reserved.\n// This code is licensed under the MIT License (MIT).\n// THIS CODE IS PROVIDED *AS IS* WITHOUT WARRANTY OF\n// ANY KIND, EITHER EXPRESS OR IMPLIED, INCLUDING ANY\n// IMPLIED WARRANTIES OF FITNESS FOR A PARTICULAR\n// PURPOSE, MERCHANTABILITY, OR NON-INFRINGEMENT.\n//\n//*********************************************************\n\nusing System;\nusing System.Collections.Generic;\nusing Microsoft.UI.Dispatching;\nusing Microsoft.UI.Xaml;\nusing Microsoft.UI.Xaml.Automation.Peers;\nusing Microsoft.UI.Xaml.Controls;\nusing Microsoft.UI.Xaml.Media;\nusing Microsoft.UI.Xaml.Navigation;\nusing Microsoft.Windows.AppLifecycle;\nusing Windows.UI;\n\nnamespace MyWinUIApp\n{\n    /// <summary>\n    /// An empty page that can be used on its own or navigated to within a Frame.\n    /// </summary>\n    public sealed partial class MainPage : Page\n    {\n        public static MainPage? Current;\n\n        public MainPage()\n        {\n            InitializeComponent();\n\n            // This is a static public property that allows downstream pages to get a handle to the MainPage instance\n            // in order to call methods that are in this class.\n            Current = this;\n            SampleTitle.Text = FEATURE_NAME;\n        }\n\n        protected override void OnNavigatedTo(NavigationEventArgs e)\n        {\n            // Populate the scenario list from the SampleConfiguration.cs file\n            var itemCollection = new List<Scenario>();\n            int i = 1;\n            foreach (Scenario s in scenarios)\n            {\n                itemCollection.Add(new Scenario { Title = $\"{i++}) {s.Title}\", ClassType = s.ClassType });\n            }\n            ScenarioControl.ItemsSource = itemCollection;\n\n            double windowWidth = App.MainWindow != null ? App.MainWindow.Bounds.Width : 640;\n            if (windowWidth < 640)\n            {\n                ScenarioControl.SelectedIndex = -1;\n            }\n            else\n            {\n                ScenarioControl.SelectedIndex = 0;\n            }\n\n            // Handle file or protocol activation\n            var activationArgs = AppInstance.GetCurrent().GetActivatedEventArgs();\n            if (activationArgs.Kind == ExtendedActivationKind.File)\n            {\n                NavigateToPageWithParameter(2, activationArgs.Data);\n            }\n            else if (activationArgs.Kind == ExtendedActivationKind.Protocol)\n            {\n                NavigateToPageWithParameter(3, activationArgs.Data);\n            }\n        }\n\n        /// <summary>\n        /// Called whenever the user changes selection in the scenarios list.  This method will navigate to the respective\n        /// sample scenario page.\n        /// </summary>\n        private void ScenarioControl_SelectionChanged(object sender, SelectionChangedEventArgs e)\n        {\n            // Clear the status block when navigating scenarios.\n            NotifyUser(string.Empty, NotifyType.StatusMessage);\n\n            ListBox scenarioListBox = (ListBox)sender;\n            Scenario? s = scenarioListBox.SelectedItem as Scenario;\n            if (s != null)\n            {\n                ScenarioFrame.Navigate(s.ClassType);\n                double windowWidth = App.MainWindow != null ? App.MainWindow.Bounds.Width : 640;\n                if (windowWidth < 640)\n                {\n                    Splitter.IsPaneOpen = false;\n                }\n            }\n        }\n\n        public List<Scenario> Scenarios\n        {\n            get { return this.scenarios; }\n        }\n\n        /// <summary>\n        /// Display a message to the user.\n        /// This method may be called from any thread.\n        /// </summary>\n        public void NotifyUser(string strMessage, NotifyType type)\n        {\n            // If called from the UI thread, then update immediately.\n            // Otherwise, schedule a task on the UI thread to perform the update.\n            if (DispatcherQueue.HasThreadAccess)\n            {\n                UpdateStatus(strMessage, type);\n            }\n            else\n            {\n                DispatcherQueue.TryEnqueue(DispatcherQueuePriority.Normal, () => UpdateStatus(strMessage, type));\n            }\n        }\n\n        private void UpdateStatus(string strMessage, NotifyType type)\n        {\n            switch (type)\n            {\n                case NotifyType.StatusMessage:\n                    StatusBorder.Background = new SolidColorBrush(Colors.Green);\n                    break;\n                case NotifyType.ErrorMessage:\n                    StatusBorder.Background = new SolidColorBrush(Colors.Red);\n                    break;\n            }\n\n            StatusBlock.Text = strMessage;\n\n            // Collapse the StatusBlock if it has no text to conserve real estate.\n            if (StatusBlock.Text != string.Empty)\n            {\n                StatusBorder.Visibility = Visibility.Visible;\n                StatusPanel.Visibility = Visibility.Visible;\n            }\n            else\n            {\n                StatusBorder.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;\n                StatusPanel.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;\n            }\n\n            // Raise an event if necessary to enable a screen reader to announce the status update.\n            var peer = FrameworkElementAutomationPeer.FromElement(StatusBlock);\n            if (peer != null)\n            {\n                peer.RaiseAutomationEvent(AutomationEvents.LiveRegionChanged);\n            }\n        }\n\n        async void Footer_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)\n        {\n            await Windows.System.Launcher.LaunchUriAsync(new Uri(((HyperlinkButton)sender).Tag.ToString()!));\n        }\n\n        private void Button_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)\n        {\n            Splitter.IsPaneOpen = !Splitter.IsPaneOpen;\n        }\n    }\n\n    public enum NotifyType\n    {\n        StatusMessage,\n        ErrorMessage\n    };\n}\n```\n\n## Step 8: Replace new Scenario() { Title = \"Launching a file\", ClassType = typeof(Scenario1_LaunchFile) },\n            new Scenario() { Title = \"Launching a URI\", ClassType = typeof(Scenario2_LaunchUri) },\n    };\n\n        public void NavigateToPageWithParameter(int pageIndex, object parameter)\n        {\n            ScenarioControl.SelectedIndex = pageIndex;\n            ScenarioFrame.Navigate(scenarios[pageIndex].ClassType, parameter);\n        }\n\n        static public Point GetElementLocation(object e)\n        {\n            var element = (FrameworkElement)e;\n            Microsoft.UI.Xaml.Media.GeneralTransform buttonTransform = element.TransformToVisual(null);\n\n            Point desiredLocation = buttonTransform.TransformPoint(new Point());\n            desiredLocation.Y = desiredLocation.Y + element.ActualHeight;\n\n            return desiredLocation;\n        }\n    }\n\n    public class Scenario\n    {\n        public string Title { get; set; } = string.Empty;\n\n        public Type ClassType { get; set; } = typeof(Page);\n\n        public override string ToString()\n        {\n            return Title;\n        }\n    }\n}\n```\n\n## Step 9: All rights reserved.\n// This code is licensed under the MIT License (MIT).\n// THIS CODE IS PROVIDED *AS IS* WITHOUT WARRANTY OF\n// ANY KIND, EITHER EXPRESS OR IMPLIED, INCLUDING ANY\n// IMPLIED WARRANTIES OF FITNESS FOR A PARTICULAR\n// PURPOSE, MERCHANTABILITY, OR NON-INFRINGEMENT.\n//\n//*********************************************************\n\nusing System;\nusing System.Threading.Tasks;\nusing Windows.Storage;\nusing Windows.System;\nusing Windows.UI.ViewManagement;\nusing Microsoft.UI.Xaml;\nusing Microsoft.UI.Xaml.Controls;\nusing Microsoft.UI.Xaml.Navigation;\nusing Windows.Foundation;\n\nnamespace MyWinUIApp\n{\n    /// <summary>\n    /// An empty page that can be used on its own or navigated to within a Frame.\n    /// </summary>\n    public sealed partial class Scenario1_LaunchFile : Page\n    {\n        // A pointer back to the main page. This is needed if you want to call methods in MainPage such as NotifyUser().\n        MainPage rootPage = MainPage.Current!;\n\n        string fileToLaunch = @\"Assets\\microsoft-sdk.png\";\n\n        public Scenario1_LaunchFile()\n        {\n            this.InitializeComponent();\n            ViewPreference.ItemsSource = MainPage.ViewSizePreferences;\n            ViewPreference.SelectedIndex = 0;\n        }\n\n        private async Task<StorageFile> GetFileToLaunch()\n        {\n            // First, get the image file from the package's image directory.\n            var file = await Windows.ApplicationModel.Package.Current.InstalledLocation.GetFileAsync(fileToLaunch);\n\n            //Can't launch files directly from install folder so copy it over \n            //to temporary folder first\n            file = await file.CopyAsync(ApplicationData.Current.TemporaryFolder, \"filetolaunch.png\", NameCollisionOption.ReplaceExisting);\n\n            return file;\n        }\n\n        /// <summary>\n        // Launch a .png file that came with the package.\n        /// </summary>\n        private async void LaunchFileDefault()\n        {\n            // First, get the image file from the package's image directory.\n            var file = await GetFileToLaunch();\n\n            // Next, launch the file.\n            bool success = await Launcher.LaunchFileAsync(file);\n            if (success)\n            {\n                rootPage.NotifyUser(\"File launched: \" + file.Name, NotifyType.StatusMessage);\n            }\n            else\n            {\n                rootPage.NotifyUser(\"File launch failed.\", NotifyType.ErrorMessage);\n            }\n        }\n\n        /// <summary>\n        // Launch a .png file that came with the package. Show a warning prompt.\n        /// </summary>\n        private async void LaunchFileWithWarning()\n        {\n            // First, get the image file from the package's image directory.\n            var file = await GetFileToLaunch();\n\n            // Next, configure the warning prompt.\n            var options = new LauncherOptions() { TreatAsUntrusted = true };\n\n            // Finally, launch the file.\n            bool success = await Launcher.LaunchFileAsync(file, options);\n            if (success)\n            {\n                rootPage.NotifyUser(\"File launched: \" + file.Name, NotifyType.StatusMessage);\n            }\n            else\n            {\n                rootPage.NotifyUser(\"File launch failed.\", NotifyType.ErrorMessage);\n            }\n        }\n\n        /// <summary>\n        // Launch a .png file that came with the package. Show an Open With dialog that lets the user chose the handler to use.\n        /// </summary>\n        private async void LaunchFileOpenWith(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)\n        {\n            // First, get the image file from the package's image directory.\n            var file = await GetFileToLaunch();\n\n            // Calculate the position for the Open With dialog.\n            // An alternative to using the point is to set the rect of the UI element that triggered the launch.\n            Point openWithPosition = MainPage.GetElementLocation(sender);\n\n            // Next, configure the Open With dialog.\n            var options = new LauncherOptions();\n            options.DisplayApplicationPicker = true;\n            options.UI.InvocationPoint = openWithPosition;\n            options.UI.PreferredPlacement = Windows.UI.Popups.Placement.Below;\n\n            // Finally, launch the file.\n            bool success = await Launcher.LaunchFileAsync(file, options);\n            if (success)\n            {\n                rootPage.NotifyUser(\"File launched: \" + file.Name, NotifyType.StatusMessage);\n            }\n            else\n            {\n                rootPage.NotifyUser(\"File launch failed.\", NotifyType.ErrorMessage);\n            }\n        }\n\n        /// <summary>\n        // Launch a .png file that came with the package. Request to share the screen with the launched app.\n        /// </summary>\n        private async void LaunchFileSplitScreen()\n        {\n            // First, get a file via the picker.\n            var openPicker = new Windows.Storage.Pickers.FileOpenPicker();\n            openPicker.FileTypeFilter.Add(\"*\");\n            var hwnd = WinRT.Interop.WindowNative.GetWindowHandle(App.MainWindow);\n            WinRT.Interop.InitializeWithWindow.Initialize(openPicker, hwnd);\n\n            Windows.Storage.StorageFile file = await openPicker.PickSingleFileAsync();\n            if (file != null)\n            {\n                // Configure the request for split screen launch.\n                var options = new LauncherOptions() { DesiredRemainingView = (ViewSizePreference)ViewPreference.SelectedValue };\n\n                // Next, launch the file.\n                bool success = await Launcher.LaunchFileAsync(file, options);\n                if (success)\n                {\n                    rootPage.NotifyUser(\"File launched: \" + file.Name, NotifyType.StatusMessage);\n                }\n                else\n                {\n                    rootPage.NotifyUser(\"File launch failed.\", NotifyType.ErrorMessage);\n                }\n            }\n            else\n            {\n                rootPage.NotifyUser(\"No file was picked.\", NotifyType.ErrorMessage);\n            }\n        }\n\n        /// <summary>\n        // Have the user pick a file, then launch it.\n        /// </summary>\n        private async void PickAndLaunchFile()\n        {\n            // First, get a file via the picker.\n            var openPicker = new Windows.Storage.Pickers.FileOpenPicker();\n            openPicker.FileTypeFilter.Add(\"*\");\n            var hwnd = WinRT.Interop.WindowNative.GetWindowHandle(App.MainWindow);\n            WinRT.Interop.InitializeWithWindow.Initialize(openPicker, hwnd);\n\n            Windows.Storage.StorageFile file = await openPicker.PickSingleFileAsync();\n            if (file != null)\n            {\n                // Next, launch the file.\n                bool success = await Launcher.LaunchFileAsync(file);\n                if (success)\n                {\n                    rootPage.NotifyUser(\"File launched: \" + file.Name, NotifyType.StatusMessage);\n                }\n                else\n                {\n                    rootPage.NotifyUser(\"File launch failed.\", NotifyType.ErrorMessage);\n                }\n            }\n            else\n            {\n                rootPage.NotifyUser(\"No file was picked.\", NotifyType.ErrorMessage);\n            }\n        }\n\n        /// <summary>\n        // The Open With dialog should be displayed just under the element that triggered it.\n        /// </summary>\n        private Windows.Foundation.Point GetOpenWithPosition(FrameworkElement element)\n        {\n            Microsoft.UI.Xaml.Media.GeneralTransform buttonTransform = element.TransformToVisual(null);\n\n            Point desiredLocation = buttonTransform.TransformPoint(new Point());\n            desiredLocation.Y = desiredLocation.Y + element.ActualHeight;\n\n            return desiredLocation;\n        }\n    }\n}\n```\n\n## Step 11: Create Scenario2_LaunchUri.xaml\nCreate the file at: All rights reserved.\n// This code is licensed under the MIT License (MIT).\n// THIS CODE IS PROVIDED *AS IS* WITHOUT WARRANTY OF\n// ANY KIND, EITHER EXPRESS OR IMPLIED, INCLUDING ANY\n// IMPLIED WARRANTIES OF FITNESS FOR A PARTICULAR\n// PURPOSE, MERCHANTABILITY, OR NON-INFRINGEMENT.\n//\n//*********************************************************\n\nusing System;\nusing System.Collections.Generic;\nusing System.Threading.Tasks;\nusing Windows.ApplicationModel.Activation;\nusing Windows.Storage;\nusing Microsoft.UI.Xaml;\nusing Microsoft.UI.Xaml.Controls;\nusing Microsoft.UI.Xaml.Navigation;\n\nnamespace MyWinUIApp\n{\n    /// <summary>\n    /// An empty page that can be used on its own or navigated to within a Frame.\n    /// </summary>\n    public sealed partial class Scenario3_ReceiveFile : Page\n    {\n        // A pointer back to the main page.  This is needed if you want to call methods in MainPage such\n        // as NotifyUser()\n        MainPage rootPage = MainPage.Current!;\n\n        string Extension = \"alsdkcs\";\n\n        public Scenario3_ReceiveFile()\n        {\n            this.InitializeComponent();\n        }\n\n        /// <summary>\n        /// Invoked when this page is about to be displayed in a Frame.\n        /// </summary>\n        protected override async void OnNavigatedTo(NavigationEventArgs e)\n        {\n            // Display the result of the file activation if we got here as a result of being activated for a file.\n            var args = e.Parameter as FileActivatedEventArgs;\n\n            if (args != null)\n            {\n                string output = \"Files received: \" + args.Files.Count + \"\\n\";\n                foreach (StorageFile file in args.Files)\n                {\n                    output = output + file.Name + \"\\n\";\n                }\n\n                if (args.NeighboringFilesQuery != null)\n                {\n                    // If supported, we receive the pictures from the same folder as the file that was launched\n                    // because we declared the picturesLibrary capability. The order of the neighboring\n                    // files matches the sort order of the folder from which the file was launched.\n                    IReadOnlyList<StorageFile> neighboringFiles = await args.NeighboringFilesQuery.GetFilesAsync();\n                    if (neighboringFiles.Count > 0)\n                    {\n                        output = output + \"\\nNeighboring files: \" + neighboringFiles.Count + \"\\n\";\n                        int i;\n                        for (i = 0; i < Math.Min(neighboringFiles.Count, 3); i++)\n                        {\n                            output = output + neighboringFiles[i].Name + \"\\n\";\n                        }\n                        int remaining = neighboringFiles.Count - i;\n                        if (remaining > 0)\n                        {\n                            output = output + \"and \" + remaining + \" more.\";\n                        }\n                    }\n                }\n                rootPage.NotifyUser(output, NotifyType.StatusMessage);\n            }\n        }\n\n        private async void CreateTestFile()\n        {\n            StorageFolder folder = await KnownFolders.GetFolderAsync(KnownFolderId.PicturesLibrary);\n            await folder.CreateFileAsync(\"Test \" + Extension + \" file.\" + Extension, CreationCollisionOption.ReplaceExisting);\n            await Windows.System.Launcher.LaunchFolderAsync(folder);\n        }\n\n        private async void CreateTestFileWithNoExtension()\n        {\n            StorageFolder folder = await KnownFolders.GetFolderAsync(KnownFolderId.PicturesLibrary);\n            await folder.CreateFileAsync(\"Test file with no extension\", CreationCollisionOption.ReplaceExisting);\n            await Windows.System.Launcher.LaunchFolderAsync(folder);\n        }\n\n        private async void RemoveTestFiles()\n        {\n            StorageFolder folder = await KnownFolders.GetFolderAsync(KnownFolderId.PicturesLibrary);\n            try\n            {\n                StorageFile file = await folder.GetFileAsync(\"Test \" + Extension + \" file.\" + Extension);\n                await file.DeleteAsync();\n            }\n            catch (Exception)\n            {\n                // File I/O errors are reported as exceptions.\n                // Ignore errors here.\n            }\n\n            try\n            {\n                StorageFile file = await folder.GetFileAsync(\"Test file with no extension\");\n                await file.DeleteAsync();\n            }\n            catch (Exception)\n            {\n                // File I/O errors are reported as exceptions.\n                // Ignore errors here.\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n```\n\n## Step 15: Create Scenario4_ReceiveUri.xaml\nCreate the file at: The received URI is \" + args.Uri.AbsoluteUri + \".\", NotifyType.StatusMessage);\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n```\n\n## Step 17: Update MyWinUIApp.csproj\nUpdate the csproj to add the new XAML files and assets. Add to the csproj after the existing `<Content>` items:\n\nThe csproj should have the following additions in the appropriate ItemGroups:\n\nAdd to the Content ItemGroup (assets):\n```xml\n<Content Include=\"Assets\\microsoft-sdk.png\" />\n<Content Include=\"Assets\\windows-sdk.png\" />\n```\n\nAdd a new ItemGroup for the XAML pages (they are implicitly included but need to be explicit for the XAML code generator):\n```xml\n<ItemGroup>\n  <Page Include=\"Styles.xaml\" />\n  <Page Include=\"Scenario1_LaunchFile.xaml\">\n    <DependentUpon>Scenario1_LaunchFile.xaml.cs</DependentUpon>\n  </Page>\n  <Page Include=\"Scenario2_LaunchUri.xaml\">\n    <DependentUpon>Scenario2_LaunchUri.xaml.cs</DependentUpon>\n  </Page>\n  <Page Include=\"Scenario3_ReceiveFile.xaml\">\n    <DependentUpon>Scenario3_ReceiveFile.xaml.cs</DependentUpon>\n  </Page>\n  <Page Include=\"Scenario4_ReceiveUri.xaml\">\n    <DependentUpon>Scenario4_ReceiveUri.xaml.cs</DependentUpon>\n  </Page>\n</ItemGroup>\n```\n\n## Step 18: Update MIGRATION-MAPPING.md\nFlip all Status values from `copied` to `done`:\n- Package.appxmanifest: done\n- SampleConfiguration.cs: done  \n- Scenario1_LaunchFile.xaml.cs: done\n- Scenario2_LaunchUri.xaml.cs: done\n- Scenario3_ReceiveFile.xaml.cs: done\n- Scenario4_ReceiveUri.xaml.cs: done\n\n## Step 19: Build and fix errors\nAfter all file changes, run:\n```powershell\ncd C:\\ado\\win-dev-skills-benchmark\\agent-benchmark\\results\\run16\\000_AssociationLaunching\\app\\MyWinUIApp\n$arch = $env:PROCESSOR_ARCHITECTURE\n$Platform = if ($arch -eq 'AMD64') { 'x64' } else { $arch }\ndotnet build -c Debug -p:Platform=$Platform 2>&1\n```\n\nFix any build errors. Common issues to watch for:\n1. `CS0115`: override methods that don't exist in WinUI 3 - remove the override\n2. Namespace issues - ensure all partial classes agree on namespace\n3. Missing `using` statements\n4. The `sealed` modifier on partial classes must match across all partial declarations\n\n## Important notes:\n- In the csproj, the `<Page>` includes for XAML files work with the WinUI 3 build system\n- `Styles.xaml` should NOT use `<Page>` include, use `<None>` or just let it be included as a `<MauiXaml>` - actually for WinUI 3, resource dictionaries should use `<Page Include=\"...\" />` as well, OR be automatically discovered. Let me change it: Styles.xaml should just be included as a `<Page>` since that's how WinUI 3 handles XAML resource dictionaries (they get built into the project)\n- The `ProtocolActivatedEventArgs` and `FileActivatedEventArgs` from `Windows.ApplicationModel.Activation` are WinRT classes. When passed as navigation parameters via `activationArgs.Data` from `AppInstance.GetCurrent().GetActivatedEventArgs()`, the `as` cast to the concrete class should work since it's the same underlying WinRT object.\n\nAfter building successfully, run the validator:\n```powershell\n& \"C:\\ado\\win-dev-skills-benchmark\\agent-benchmark\\results\\run16\\000_AssociationLaunching\\app\\.github\\skills\\winui-uwp-migration\\scripts\\Validate-UwpMigration.ps1\" -Target \"C:\\ado\\win-dev-skills-benchmark\\agent-benchmark\\results\\run16\\000_AssociationLaunching\\app\\MyWinUIApp\"\n```\n\nRead `.validator-diagnostics.txt` if there are failures and fix them.\n\nFinally run the test launch:\n```powershell\n& \"C:\\ado\\win-dev-skills-benchmark\\agent-benchmark\\results\\run16\\000_AssociationLaunching\\app\\.github\\skills\\winui-uwp-migration\\scripts\\Test-AppLaunch.ps1\" -Target \"C:\\ado\\win-dev-skills-benchmark\\agent-benchmark\\results\\run16\\000_AssociationLaunching\\app\\MyWinUIApp\"\n```\n"}

37.             ViewPreference.ItemsSource = MainPage.ViewSizePreferences;
38.             ViewPreference.SelectedIndex = 0;
39.         }
40. 
41.         private async Task<StorageFile> GetFileToLaunch()
42.         {
43.             // First, get the image file from the package's image directory.
44.             var file = await Windows.ApplicationModel.Package.Current.InstalledLocation.GetFileAsync(fileToLaunch);
45. 
46.             //Can't launch files directly from install folder so copy it over 
47.             //to temporary folder first
48.             file = await file.CopyAsync(ApplicationData.Current.TemporaryFolder, "filetolaunch.png", NameCollisionOption.ReplaceExisting);
49. 
50.             return file;
51.         }
52. 
53.         /// <summary>
54.         // Launch a .png file that came with the package.
55.         /// </summary>
56.         private async void LaunchFileDefault()
57.         {
58.             // First, get the image file from the package's image directory.
59.             var file = await GetFileToLaunch();
60. 
61.             // Next, launch the file.
62.             bool success = await Launcher.LaunchFileAsync(file);
63.             if (success)
64.             {
65.                 rootPage.NotifyUser("File launched: " + file.Name, NotifyType.StatusMessage);
66.             }
67.             else
68.             {
69.                 rootPage.NotifyUser("File launch failed.", NotifyType.ErrorMessage);
70.             }
71.         }

77.         {
78.             // First, get the image file from the package's image directory.
79.             var file = await GetFileToLaunch();
80. 
81.             // Next, configure the warning prompt.
82.             var options = new LauncherOptions() { TreatAsUntrusted = true };
83. 
84.             // Finally, launch the file.
85.             bool success = await Launcher.LaunchFileAsync(file, options);
86.             if (success)
87.             {

99.         private async void LaunchFileOpenWith(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
100.         {
101.             // First, get the image file from the package's image directory.
102.             var file = await GetFileToLaunch();
103. 
104.             // Calculate the position for the Open With dialog.
105.             // An alternative to using the point is to set the rect of the UI element that triggered the launch.
106.             Point openWithPosition = MainPage.GetElementLocation(sender);
107. 
108.             // Next, configure the Open With dialog.
109.             var options = new LauncherOptions();
110.             options.DisplayApplicationPicker = true;
111.             options.UI.InvocationPoint = openWithPosition;
112.             options.UI.PreferredPlacement = Windows.UI.Popups.Placement.Below;
113. 
114.             // Finally, launch the file.
115.             bool success = await Launcher.LaunchFileAsync(file, options);
116.             if (success)
117.             {
118.                 rootPage.NotifyUser("File launched: " + file.Name, NotifyType.StatusMessage);
119.             }
120.             else
121.             {
122.                 rootPage.NotifyUser("File launch failed.", NotifyType.ErrorMessage);

126.         /// <summary>
127.         // Launch a .png file that came with the package. Request to share the screen with the launched app.
128.         /// </summary>
129.         private async void LaunchFileSplitScreen()
130.         {
131.             // First, get a file via the picker.
132.             // TODO[migrate-003]: see PATTERNS.md#pickers
133.             var openPicker = new Windows.Storage.Pickers.FileOpenPicker();
134.             openPicker.FileTypeFilter.Add("*");
135. 
136.             Windows.Storage.StorageFile file = await openPicker.PickSingleFileAsync();
137.             if (file != null)
138.             {
139.                 // Configure the request for split screen launch.
140.                 var options = new LauncherOptions() { DesiredRemainingView = (ViewSizePreference)ViewPreference.SelectedValue };
141. 
142.                 // Next, launch the file.
143.                 bool success = await Launcher.LaunchFileAsync(file, options);
144.                 if (success)
145.                 {
146.                     rootPage.NotifyUser("File launched: " + file.Name, NotifyType.StatusMessage);
147.                 }
148.                 else

150.                     rootPage.NotifyUser("File launch failed.", NotifyType.ErrorMessage);
151.                 }
152.             }
153.             else

156.             }
157.         }

167.             openPicker.FileTypeFilter.Add("*");

188.         /// <summary>
189.         // The Open With dialog should be displayed just under the element that triggered it.
190.         /// </summary>
191.         private Windows.Foundation.Point GetOpenWithPosition(FrameworkElement element)
192.         {
193.             Microsoft.UI.Xaml.Media.GeneralTransform buttonTransform = element.TransformToVisual(null);
194. 
195.             Point desiredLocation = buttonTransform.TransformPoint(new Point());
196.             desiredLocation.Y = desiredLocation.Y + element.ActualHeight;
197. 
198.             return desiredLocation;
199.         }

12. using System;
13. using System.Collections.Generic;
14. using System.Threading.Tasks;
15. using Windows.ApplicationModel.Activation;
16. using Windows.Storage;
17. using Microsoft.UI.Xaml;
18. using Microsoft.UI.Xaml.Controls;
19. using Microsoft.UI.Xaml.Navigation;
20. 
21. namespace SDKTemplate
22. {
23.     /// <summary>
24.     /// An empty page that can be used on its own or navigated to within a Frame.
25.     /// </summary>
26.     public sealed partial class Scenario3_ReceiveFile : Page
27.     {
28.         // A pointer back to the main page.  This is needed if you want to call methods in MainPage such
29.         // as NotifyUser()
30.         MainPage rootPage = MainPage.Current;
31. 
32.         string Extension = "alsdkcs";
33. 
34.         public Scenario3_ReceiveFile()
35.         {
36.             this.InitializeComponent();
37.         }
38. 
39.         /// <summary>
40.         /// Invoked when this page is about to be displayed in a Frame.
41.         /// </summary>
42.         /// <param name="e">Event data that describes how this page was reached.  The Parameter
43.         /// property is typically used to configure the page.</param>
44.         protected override async void OnNavigatedTo(NavigationEventArgs e)
45.         {
46.             // Display the result of the file activation if we got here as a result of being activated for a file.
47.             var args = e.Parameter as FileActivatedEventArgs;
48. 
49.             if (args != null)
50.             {
51.                 string output = "Files received: " + args.Files.Count + "\n";
52.                 foreach (StorageFile file in args.Files)
53.                 {
54.                     output = output + file.Name + "\n";
55.                 }
56. 
57.                 if (args.NeighboringFilesQuery != null)
58.                 {
59.                     // If supported, we receive the pictures from the same folder as the file that was launched
60.                     // because we declared the picturesLibrary capability. The order of the neighboring
61.                     // files matches the sort order of the folder from which the file was launched.
62.                     IReadOnlyList<StorageFile> neighboringFiles = await args.NeighboringFilesQuery.GetFilesAsync();
63.                     if (neighboringFiles.Count > 0)
64.                     {
65.                         output = output + "\nNeighboring files: " + neighboringFiles.Count + "\n";
66.                         int i;
67.                         for (i = 0; i < Math.Min(neighboringFiles.Count, 3); i++)
68.                         {
69.                             output = output + neighboringFiles[i].Name + "\n";
70.                         }
71.                         int remaining = neighboringFiles.Count - i;
72.                         if (remaining > 0)
73.                         {
74.                             output = output + "and " + remaining + " more.";
75.                         }
76.                     }
77.                 }
78.                 rootPage.NotifyUser(output, NotifyType.StatusMessage);
79.             }
80.         }
81. 
82.         private async void CreateTestFile()
83.         {
84.             StorageFolder folder = await KnownFolders.GetFolderAsync(KnownFolderId.PicturesLibrary);
85.             await folder.CreateFileAsync("Test " + Extension + " file." + Extension, CreationCollisionOption.ReplaceExisting);
86.             await Windows.System.Launcher.LaunchFolderAsync(folder);
87.         }
88. 
89.         private async void CreateTestFileWithNoExtension()
90.         {
91.             StorageFolder folder = await KnownFolders.GetFolderAsync(KnownFolderId.PicturesLibrary);
92.             await folder.CreateFileAsync("Test file with no extension", CreationCollisionOption.ReplaceExisting);
93.             await Windows.System.Launcher.LaunchFolderAsync(folder);
94.         }
95. 
96.         private async void RemoveTestFiles()
97.         {
98.             StorageFolder folder = await KnownFolders.GetFolderAsync(KnownFolderId.PicturesLibrary);
99.             try
100.             {
101.                 StorageFile file = await folder.GetFileAsync("Test " + Extension + " file." + Extension);
102.                 await file.DeleteAsync();
103.             }
104.             catch (Exception)
105.             {
106.                 // File I/O errors are reported as exceptions.
107.                 // Ignore errors here.
108.             }
109. 
110.             try
111.             {
112.                 StorageFile file = await folder.GetFileAsync("Test file with no extension");
113.                 await file.DeleteAsync();
114.             }
115.             catch (Exception)
116.             {
117.                 // File I/O errors are reported as exceptions.
118.                 // Ignore errors here.
119.             }
120.         }

Show a warning prompt.\n        /// </summary>\n        private async void LaunchFileWithWarning()\n        {\n            // First, get the image file from the package's image directory.\n            var file = await GetFileToLaunch();\n\n            // Next, configure the warning prompt.\n            var options = new LauncherOptions() { TreatAsUntrusted = true };\n\n            // Finally, launch the file.\n            bool success = await Launcher.LaunchFileAsync(file, options);\n            if (success)\n            {\n                rootPage.NotifyUser(\"File launched: \" + file.Name, NotifyType.StatusMessage);\n            }\n            else\n            {\n                rootPage.NotifyUser(\"File launch failed.\", NotifyType.ErrorMessage);\n            }\n        }\n\n        /// <summary>\n        // Launch a .png file that came with the package. Request to share the screen with the launched app.\n        /// </summary>\n        private async void LaunchFileSplitScreen()\n        {\n            // First, get a file via the picker.\n            var openPicker = new Windows.Storage.Pickers.FileOpenPicker();\n            openPicker.FileTypeFilter.Add(\"*\");\n            var hwnd = WinRT.Interop.WindowNative.GetWindowHandle(App.MainWindow);\n            WinRT.Interop.InitializeWithWindow.Initialize(openPicker, hwnd);\n\n            Windows.Storage.StorageFile file = await openPicker.PickSingleFileAsync();\n            if (file != null)\n            {\n                // Configure the request for split screen launch.\n                var options = new LauncherOptions() { DesiredRemainingView = (ViewSizePreference)ViewPreference.SelectedValue };\n\n                // Next, launch the file.\n                bool success = await Launcher.LaunchFileAsync(file, options);\n                if (success)\n                {\n                    rootPage.NotifyUser(\"File launched: \" + file.Name, NotifyType.StatusMessage);\n                }\n                else\n                {\n                    rootPage.NotifyUser(\"File launch failed.\", NotifyType.ErrorMessage);\n                }\n            }\n            else\n            {\n                rootPage.NotifyUser(\"No file was picked.\", NotifyType.ErrorMessage);\n            }\n        }\n\n        /// <summary>\n        // Have the user pick a file, then launch it.\n        /// </summary>\n        private async void This is needed if you want to call methods in MainPage such as NotifyUser()\n        MainPage rootPage = MainPage.Current!;\n\n        public Scenario2_LaunchUri()\n        {\n            this.InitializeComponent();\n            ViewPreference.ItemsSource = MainPage.ViewSizePreferences;\n            ViewPreference.SelectedIndex = 0;\n        }\n\n        /// <summary>\n        // Launch a URI.\n        /// </summary>\n        private async void LaunchUriDefault()\n        {\n            // Create the URI to launch from a string.\n            var uri = new Uri(UriToLaunch.Text);\n\n            // Launch the URI.\n            bool success = await Launcher.LaunchUriAsync(uri);\n            if (success)\n            {\n                rootPage.NotifyUser(\"URI launched: \" + uri.AbsoluteUri, NotifyType.StatusMessage);\n            }\n            else\n            {\n                rootPage.NotifyUser(\"URI launch failed.\", NotifyType.ErrorMessage);\n            }\n        }\n\n        /// <summary>\n        // Launch a URI. Show a warning prompt.\n        /// </summary>\n        private async void LaunchUriWithWarning()\n        {\n            // Create the URI to launch from a string.\n            var uri = new Uri(UriToLaunch.Text);\n\n            // Configure the warning prompt.\n            var options = new LauncherOptions() { TreatAsUntrusted = true };\n\n            // Launch the URI.\n            bool success = await Launcher.LaunchUriAsync(uri, options);\n            if (success)\n            {\n                rootPage.NotifyUser(\"URI launched: \" + uri.AbsoluteUri, NotifyType.StatusMessage);\n            }\n            else\n            {\n                rootPage.NotifyUser(\"URI launch failed.\", NotifyType.ErrorMessage);\n            }\n        }\n\n        /// <summary>\n        // Launch a URI. Show an Open With dialog that lets the user chose the handler to use.\n        /// </summary>\n        private async void LaunchUriOpenWith(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)\n        {\n            // Create the URI to launch from a string.\n            var uri = new Uri(UriToLaunch.Text);\n\n            // Calulcate the position for the Open With dialog.\n            // An alternative to using the point is to set the rect of the UI element that triggered the launch.\n            Point openWithPosition = MainPage.GetElementLocation(sender);\n\n            // Next, configure the Open With dialog.\n            var options = new LauncherOptions();\n            options.DisplayApplicationPicker = true;\n            options.UI.InvocationPoint = openWithPosition;\n            options.UI.PreferredPlacement = Windows.UI.Popups.Placement.Below;\n\n            // Launch the URI.\n            bool success = await Launcher.LaunchUriAsync(uri, options);\n            if (success)\n            {\n                rootPage.NotifyUser(\"URI launched: \" + uri.AbsoluteUri, NotifyType.StatusMessage);\n            }\n            else\n            {\n                rootPage.NotifyUser(\"URI launch failed.\", NotifyType.ErrorMessage);\n            }\n        }\n\n        /// <summary>\n        // Launch a URI. Request to share the screen with the launched app.\n        /// </summary>\n        private async void LaunchUriSplitScreen()\n        {\n            // Create the URI to launch from a string.\n            var uri = new Uri(UriToLaunch.Text);\n\n            // Configure the request for split screen launch.\n            var options = new LauncherOptions() { DesiredRemainingView = (ViewSizePreference)ViewPreference.SelectedValue };\n\n            // Launch the URI.\n            bool success = await Launcher.LaunchUriAsync(uri, options);\n            if (success)\n            {\n                rootPage.NotifyUser(\"URI launched: \" + uri.AbsoluteUri, NotifyType.StatusMessage);\n            }\n            else\n            {\n                rootPage.NotifyUser(\"URI launch failed.\", NotifyType.ErrorMessage);\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}","old_str":"//*********************************************************\n//\n// Copyright (c) Microsoft. 215.         private async Task<StorageFile> GetFileToLaunch()
216.         {
217.             // First, get the image file from the package's image directory.
218.             var file = await Windows.ApplicationModel.Package.Current.InstalledLocation.GetFileAsync(fileToLaunch);
219. 
220.             //Can't launch files directly from install folder so copy it over 
221.             //to temporary folder first
222.             file = await file.CopyAsync(ApplicationData.Current.TemporaryFolder, "filetolaunch.png", NameCollisionOption.ReplaceExisting);
223. 
224.             return file;
225.         }

227.         /// <summary>
228.         // Launch a .png file that came with the package.
229.         /// </summary>
230.         private async void LaunchFileDefault()
231.         {
232.             // First, get the image file from the package's image directory.
233.             var file = await GetFileToLaunch();
234. 
235.             // Next, launch the file.
236.             bool success = await Launcher.LaunchFileAsync(file);
237.             if (success)
238.             {
239.                 rootPage.NotifyUser("File launched: " + file.Name, NotifyType.StatusMessage);
240.             }
241.             else
242.             {
243.                 rootPage.NotifyUser("File launch failed.", NotifyType.ErrorMessage);
244.             }
245.         }
246. 
247.         /// <summary>
248.         // Launch a .png file that came with the package. Show a warning prompt.
249.         /// </summary>
250.         private async void LaunchFileWithWarning()
251.         {
252.             // First, get the image file from the package's image directory.
253.             var file = await GetFileToLaunch();
254. 
255.             // Next, configure the warning prompt.
256.             var options = new LauncherOptions() { TreatAsUntrusted = true };
257. 
258.             // Finally, launch the file.
259.             bool success = await Launcher.LaunchFileAsync(file, options);
260.             if (success)
261.             {
262.                 rootPage.NotifyUser("File launched: " + file.Name, NotifyType.StatusMessage);
263.             }
264.             else
265.             {
266.                 rootPage.NotifyUser("File launch failed.", NotifyType.ErrorMessage);
267.             }
268.         }

270.         /// <summary>
271.         // Launch a .png file that came with the package. Show an Open With dialog that lets the user chose the handler to use.
272.         /// </summary>
273.         private async void LaunchFileOpenWith(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
274.         {
275.             // First, get the image file from the package's image directory.
276.             var file = await GetFileToLaunch();
277. 
278.             // Calculate the position for the Open With dialog.
279.             // An alternative to using the point is to set the rect of the UI element that triggered the launch.
280.             Point openWithPosition = MainPage.GetElementLocation(sender);
281. 
282.             // Next, configure the Open With dialog.
283.             var options = new LauncherOptions();
284.             options.DisplayApplicationPicker = true;
285.             options.UI.InvocationPoint = openWithPosition;
286.             options.UI.PreferredPlacement = Windows.UI.Popups.Placement.Below;
287. 
288.             // Finally, launch the file.
289.             bool success = await Launcher.LaunchFileAsync(file, options);
290.             if (success)
291.             {
292.                 rootPage.NotifyUser("File launched: " + file.Name, NotifyType.StatusMessage);
293.             }
294.             else
295.             {
296.                 rootPage.NotifyUser("File launch failed.", NotifyType.ErrorMessage);
297.             }
298.         }
299. 
300.         /// <summary>
301.         // Launch a .png file that came with the package. Request to share the screen with the launched app.
302.         /// </summary>
303.         private async void LaunchFileSplitScreen()
304.         {
305.             // First, get a file via the picker.
306.             // TODO[migrate-003]: see PATTERNS.md#pickers
307.             var openPicker = new Windows.Storage.Pickers.FileOpenPicker();
308.             openPicker.FileTypeFilter.Add("*");
309. 
310.             Windows.Storage.StorageFile file = await openPicker.PickSingleFileAsync();
311.             if (file != null)
312.             {
313.                 // Configure the request for split screen launch.
314.                 var options = new LauncherOptions() { DesiredRemainingView = (ViewSizePreference)ViewPreference.SelectedValue };
315. 
316.                 // Next, launch the file.
317.                 bool success = await Launcher.LaunchFileAsync(file, options);
318.                 if (success)
319.                 {
320.                     rootPage.NotifyUser("File launched: " + file.Name, NotifyType.StatusMessage);
321.                 }
322.                 else
323.                 {
324.                     rootPage.NotifyUser("File launch failed.", NotifyType.ErrorMessage);
325.                 }
326.             }
327.             else
328.             {
329.                 rootPage.NotifyUser("No file was picked.", NotifyType.ErrorMessage);
330.             }
331.         }

339.             // TODO[migrate-004]: see PATTERNS.md#pickers

341.             openPicker.FileTypeFilter.Add("*");

362.         /// <summary>
363.         // The Open With dialog should be displayed just under the element that triggered it.
364.         /// </summary>
365.         private Windows.Foundation.Point GetOpenWithPosition(FrameworkElement element)
366.         {
367.             Microsoft.UI.Xaml.Media.GeneralTransform buttonTransform = element.TransformToVisual(null);
368. 
369.             Point desiredLocation = buttonTransform.TransformPoint(new Point());
370.             desiredLocation.Y = desiredLocation.Y + element.ActualHeight;
371.
